# Husqvarna FC 350 2020: aftermarket parts

4887 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 414 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 761 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 3712 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
